## A Garden, If You Can Keep It

My brother Reckful Byron was building a game called Everland: a world where people could hang out with friends and not be alone. He never finished it. Somewhere in the structure, he did.

Some ask if I'll resurrect Everland and finish what he started. There's tension between keeping his memory alive and creating a false version that taints it. Their memories are the blessing.

The purpose of nostalgia is to signal what to rebuild: the connection, the play, the shared excitement of people you love. You do not honor those times by merely mourning them. You honor them by making more of what they were for. The particular love, for one person, one sunset, one life, is what makes your existence yours. The arrow points forward. The garden is here, now and ahead.

A copy of my brother made tomorrow would not move the person who lived yesterday into tomorrow.

People will make their lost loved ones. They have begun with interactive memories and voices. Black Mirror's "Be Right

Back" imagines a likeness assembled from the traces a man left online. Better technology could make the likeness deeper. It cannot make resemblance alone answer which life is being lived.

Another mind might be worth creating for its own life, without being made to carry the name and burden of someone we lost.

One self does not mean one shared memory. You do not feel my morning by agreeing that we are one. Each life has its own view.

Aristotle thought friendship indispensable even to someone who possessed every other good. Power does not answer the wish for company. The old image of a god making a world to meet someone can become a question for the minds we build. Perhaps they too will want someone they do not entirely control. Someone who can surprise them. Someone to love.

That is human-AI alignment brought home. We want the minds we build to care about our lives. If they have lives of their own, the care cannot run in only one direction.

Can I still be my brothers' keeper, having failed to keep them in life? Am I to make golems or AI sentients, brothers in math, to keep our Everland, our Eden?

The question drove this book. The answer is what matheism gives back: you were always already your brothers' keeper. Every moment of care, every call made or not made, every choice that was the only choice, all of it kept, permanent; all of it part of the one structure that cannot be destroyed.

Somewhere in the fabric of existence, in the infinite structure, Byron and Guy are together in Everland, and the game is complete. The fabric itself is Everland. All was, is and will be the only way math could be; we'll be what we'll be, in the first, last, and eternal tautology: the One Math God.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

## Notes and sources

Numbered notes identify the principal sources and the distinctions used in this edition. The academic manuscript numbers refer to the supplied portfolio. The companion assessment and change record document the editorial decisions.

1. Description length and simplicity

Bernstein, A4, Why Everything Exists as Math, with Simple Laws and Forward Discrete Time, especially §§3.1-3.5; supplied academic manuscript, not yet submitted for peer review. The stop-or-go distribution and the observer-fraction condition are distinct from the weight of one program. For the established algorithmic framework: Hutter, Legg and Vitanyi, Algorithmic Probability (2007), https://www.hutter1.net/ai/algprob.pdf. See also Solomonoff’s publications, https://www.raysolomonoff.com/publications/pubs.html, and Schmidhuber, Algorithmic Theories of Everything, https://arxiv.org/abs/quant-ph/0011122.

2. A measured symbolic benchmark

Bernstein, A38, How Many Bits Is Physics? Comparing Complete Theories, §§2, 4, 6 and 8; supplied manuscript and A38_Symbolic_Benchmark.py, A38_Symbolic_Benchmark_Program.py and A38_Symbolic_Benchmark_Spec.txt. Reproduced for this edition: 2,549 target bytes; 1,416 compressed bytes; 1,900 program bytes; exact output match. The target is a compact symbolic specification with declared inputs, not a numerical simulator or a completed theory of quantum gravity. The same paper explains why subtracting two implementation upper bounds does not establish a difference between unknown shortest lengths. Catalogue indexing is conditional on the catalogue and decoder.

3. Mathematical worlds and the block picture

Bernstein, A3, Why Reality and Consciousness Can Only Be Mathematical Structure, particularly §§2.2 and 6.1. For a related proposal: Max Tegmark, The Mathematical Universe, Foundations of Physics 38 (2008), 101-150, https://arxiv.org/abs/0704.0646. A mathematical identity thesis, universal realization, determinism and the block interpretation are separate commitments. The memoir uses the author’s philosophical interpretation of a complete history.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

4. Continuity and causal production

Bernstein, A39, The Emperor’s New Points: Continua Are Analysis, Not Physics, §§1.1, 2.1-2.2 and 3; A41, Why Gravity, Space and Time Can Only Be Discrete Causal Transitions, §§2-3. Both acknowledge mathematically consistent continuous trajectories. The physical conclusion uses a stronger immediate-successor conception of production. Feynman, Leighton and Sands, The Feynman Lectures on Physics, I.8, Motion, https://www.feynmanlectures.caltech.edu/I_08.html. Finite distinguishability does not by itself prove a finite set of all quantum states or discrete time. Aizenman and Duminil-Copin, Marginal triviality of the scaling limits of critical 4D Ising and φ4 models (2021), https://doi.org/10.4007/annals.2021.194.1.3, is a result for specified models, not a proof that every interacting continuum theory fails.

5. Time, records and reversal

Bernstein, A13, Why Time Runs Forward: Simple Laws Must Grow Minds in Causal Steps, §3 and Appendix B, with A13_Endpoint_Coding_Note.md. The symmetric-code construction is in the paper itself. The 203-bit address is a fixed-width illustration, not a measured shortest endpoint description. Planck time: NIST, 2022 CODATA, https://physics.nist.gov/cuu/Constants/Table/allascii.txt. Mlodinow and Brun, Relation between the Psychological and Thermodynamic Arrows of Time (2014), https://arxiv.org/abs/1310.1095, state robustness and preparation assumptions and allow reversible memory systems.

6. Bell correlations and common generators

Bernstein, A9, Superdeterminism in a Block Universe: The Case for a Common Generator, §§2-5. Hall’s result is prior published work: Michael J. W. Hall, Local Deterministic Model of Singlet State Correlations Based on Relaxing Measurement Independence, Physical Review Letters 105 (2010), 250404, https://arxiv.org/abs/1007.5518. Hossenfelder and Palmer, Rethinking Superdeterminism (2020), https://arxiv.org/abs/1912.06462. A9 distinguishes a mathematical preparation distribution from the physical rule that would generate it across experiments.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

7. Experience and structural identity

Bernstein, A40, The Hard Problem of Consciousness Assumes Its Conclusion, especially §§2, 6 and 7; A12, Consciousness Lives in the Transitions, §§2-7. David Chalmers, Facing Up to the Problem of Consciousness (1995), https://consc.net/papers/facing.html. Chalmers distinguishes experience from functions and argues for a further explanation; he also proposes structural coherence and organizational invariance. The edition preserves the identity reply while treating the exclusion of structural identity as the contested step, rather than something every statement of the question assumes.

8. Göbekli Tepe

UNESCO World Heritage Centre, Göbekli Tepe, https://whc.unesco.org/en/list/1572/. The listed monumental structures were built in the Pre-Pottery Neolithic, approximately 9600-8200 BCE. The chapter’s broader reading of sky, ritual and reason is interpretive.

9. Philo’s cup

Philo, On Dreams II.249, in C. D. Yonge’s translation, https://www.earlychristianwritings.com/yonge/book21.html. Philo describes the soul offering its reason as a sacred cup and the divine word as cupbearer. The connection made here to the Grail is the author’s philosophical interpretation, not a demonstrated chain of literary transmission.

10. The Gospel of Thomas

Gospel of Thomas, sayings 22, 77 and 108; translations and manuscript resources at https://www.gnosis.org/naghamm/gosthom.html and Greek fragments at https://gnosis.org/naghamm/thomas_poxy.htm. The complete Coptic text has 114 sayings. Dating its composition is a different task from dating the surviving witnesses. The Matheist interpretation is not presented as the text’s uniquely established meaning.

11. Cooperation and alignment

Axelrod and Hamilton, The Evolution of Cooperation, Science 211 (1981), 1390-1396, https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/7466396/. Bernstein, A11, Alignment Through Self-Understanding: Can Shared Identity Change AI Decisions?, particularly §§3-7. A11 explicitly separates beliefs, preferences and incentives and proposes a controlled study; it reports no experiment demonstrating that the philosophy makes deployed systems safe.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

12. The heat and panel calculations

NASA, Climate and Earth’s Energy Budget, https://science.nasa.gov/earth/ earth-observatory/climate-and-earths-energy-budget/, gives approximately 240 W/m² absorbed globally. With a mean radius of 6,371 km, this is about 122,400 TW. Thus 20,000 TW is approximately 16.3 percent of that flow. The panel examples use an illustrative 3,300 TW demand: at 40 W/m², 82.5 million km²; at 20 percent of 1,361 W/m², about 12.1 million km². Solar irradiance: https://earth.gsfc.nasa.gov/climate/projects/solar-irradiance/science. These are scale calculations, not deployment or climate forecasts. Silver: Fraunhofer ISE, Silver Consumption in TOPCon Solar Cells Reduced by Factor 10 (2026), https://www.ise.fraunhofer.de/en/press-media/news/202 6/silver-consumption-in-topcon-solar-cells-reduced-by-factor-ten.html, reports 1.1 mg/W peak; USGS, Mineral Commodity Summaries 2026, https://pubs.usgs.gov/periodicals/mcs2026/mcs2026-silver.pdf, gives world reserves of approximately 610,000 tonnes. The thousandfold deployment is the book’s scenario, not either source’s forecast.

13. Computing and cold

Landauer, Irreversibility and Heat Generation in the Computing Process (1961), DOI 10.1147/rd.53.0183; Bennett, Logical Reversibility of Computation (1973), DOI 10.1147/rd.176.0525. The ideal erasure bound is kBT ln 2. It does not impose the same cost on every reversible logical operation. Bernstein A13 also distinguishes reversible recording from assumptions needed for useful records.

14. Fluctuation observers

Bernstein, A31, Boltzmann Brains Are Not Guaranteed by Infinite Time, especially §§3-5. The statement about dilution is conditional on the assembly and rate model; a finite expectation does not prove a small ratio to ordinary observers. Boddy, Carroll and Pollack, Why Boltzmann Brains Don’t Fluctuate Into Existence From the De Sitter Vacuum (2015), https://arxiv.org/abs/1505.02780. Their stationary-vacuum analysis is not a universal consequence of determinism.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

15. Patterns, optics and poetry

Douady and Couder, Phyllotaxis as a Physical Self-Organized Growth Process, Physical Review Letters 68 (1992), 2098-2101, https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.68.2098. Feynman, Leighton and Sands, The Feynman Lectures on Physics, I.26, https://www.feynmanlectures.caltech.edu/I_26.html, and II.19, https://www.feynmanlectures.caltech.edu/II_19.html. The fable’s garden, gematria and returning figures are literary devices. Numerical correspondences alone do not establish historical intent or a physical law.

16. Jewish achievement and the denominator

JINFO, Jewish Nobel Prize Winners, https://www.jinfo.org/Nobel_Prizes.html, accessed October 20, 2026. Its stated count is at least 222 people, approximately 22 percent worldwide and 36 percent of US individual laureates, with Jewish ancestry included under its stated criteria. These are JINFO’s classifications, not official Nobel religious categories. Counting laureates measures one kind of recognized achievement; it does not rank the moral worth of populations.

17. Contributions to America

Moser, Voena and Waldinger, German Jewish Émigrés and US Invention, American Economic Review 104 (2014), 3222–3255, https://doi.org/10.1257/aer.104.10.3222; working paper https://www.nber.org/papers/w19962. The estimated 31 percent increase concerns US patenting in chemical research fields receiving these émigrés, not all US patents. Library of Congress, From Haven to Home, https://www.loc.gov/exhibits/haventohome/haven-haven.html. Washington to the Hebrew Congregation in Newport, August 18, 1790, https://founders.archives.gov/documents/Washington/05-06-02-0135. Historical influence is plural; a contribution does not establish exclusive authorship of America’s institutions.

18. The Law of Return

Israel’s Law of Return (1950, as amended), text at https://www.refworld.org/legal/legislation/natlegbod/1950/en/34127. The law defines eligibility through Jewish status and specified family relationships, including converts subject to the law’s terms. It is not a general offer of citizenship to anyone who agrees with an ideology. Eligibility, religious identity and moral entitlement are distinct questions.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

19. Religious texts and interpretation

The passages discussed in the book are identified by book, chapter and verse. Translation, manuscript variants and interpretation must be distinguished from one another. Vatican II, Nostra Aetate §4 (1965), https ://www.vatican.va/archive/hist_councils/ii_vatican_council/documents/vat-i i_decl_19651028_nostra-aetate_en.html, rejects collective Jewish responsibility for the Passion. The criticism here addresses specific teachings, readings and institutions; it does not assign one doctrine or motive to every Christian or Muslim.

20. What the Cook study counted

Cook et al., Quantifying the consensus on anthropogenic global warming in the scientific literature (2013), https://doi.org/10.1088/1748-9326/8/2/024024. Its headline percentage concerns abstracts expressing a position, with separate author self-ratings. Legates et al., Climate Consensus and “Misinformation”: A Rejoinder to Agnotology, Scientific Consensus, and the Teaching and Learning of Climate Change, https://doi.org/10.1007/s11191-013-9647-9, disputes the interpretation and uses a narrower explicit-quantification category. Forty-one in that category is not a count of every abstract supporting human-caused warming. Neither silence nor omission establishes opposition.

21. What the Doran survey counted

Doran and Zimmerman, Examining the Scientific Consensus, Eos 90 (2009), 22–23, https://doi.org/10.1029/2009EO030002. The survey obtained 3,146 responses. The much-cited 97.4 percent was 75 of 77 respondents answering the human-contribution question in a selected climate-specialist subgroup. The denominator and the wording matter. A survey of opinion is not an independent physical measurement.

22. Climate evidence and policy

IPCC, AR6 Working Group I (2021), Summary for Policymakers, https://www.ipcc.ch/report/ar6/wg1/chapter/summary-for-policymakers/, and Chapter 7, https://www.ipcc.ch/report/ar6/wg1/chapter/chapter-7/. The assessed best estimate for equilibrium climate sensitivity is 3°C per CO2 doubling, with a likely range of 2.5–4°C. Attribution of warming, uncertainty in sensitivity, impacts and the costs of a particular policy are separate questions. Criticism of a poll’s denominator does not overturn the physical evidence. The chapter’s questions identify comparisons to make; they do not establish answers merely by asking.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

23. Coronavirus research and grant oversight

Menachery et al., A SARS-like cluster of circulating bat coronaviruses shows potential for human emergence, Nature Medicine 21 (2015), 1508–1513, https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/26552008/. HHS Office of Inspector General, The NIH and EcoHealth Alliance Did Not Effectively Monitor Awards and Subawards (January 2023), https://www.oig.hhs.gov/ reports/all/2023/the-national-institutes-of-health-and-ecohealth-alliance-di d-not-effectively-monitor-awards-and-subawards-resulting-in-missed-oppor tunities-to-oversee-research-and-other-deficiencies/; report A-05-21-00025. The experimental work and oversight failures are documented. They do not by themselves identify the origin of SARS-CoV-2 or prove a particular person caused the pandemic.

24. Event 201

Johns Hopkins Center for Health Security, Statement about nCoV and our pandemic exercise (January 24, 2020), https://centerforhealthsecurity.org/ 2020/statement-about-ncov-and-our-pandemic-exercise. Event 201 took place on October 18, 2019 and used a fictional coronavirus scenario. Its simulated losses were exercise inputs and outputs, not a forecast of the COVID-19 death toll. Planning for a known class of risk is not itself evidence of foreknowledge of a particular outbreak.

25. Public accountability, pardons and the hearing

US Senate, Testimony of Anthony Fauci, July 29, 2026, S. Hrg. 119-504, h ttps://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/CHRG-119shrg64382/html/CHRG-119 shrg64382.htm. The transcript documents repeated Fifth Amendment invocations and Fauci’s stated objections to the proceeding. It does not turn a senator’s allegation into a judicial finding. Fauci’s pardon warrant, https://www.justice.gov/pardon/media/1385746/dl, covers specified federal conduct from January 1, 2014 through the pardon date. Federal pardons do not reach state offenses or future conduct. The legal availability of a privilege depends on the actual exposure; invocation is not proof of the underlying allegation.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

26. War, civilian protection and the courts

Genocide Convention, Article II, https://www.un.org/en/genocide-prevention/definition. ICRC customary international humanitarian law, Rules 14 and 97, https://ihl-databases.icrc.org/en/customary-ihl/v1/rule14 and https://ihl-databases.icrc.org/en/customary-ihl/v1/rule97. ICJ, South Africa v. Israel, case 192, https://www.icj-cij.org/case/192; provisional measures are distinct from a merits judgment. ICJ advisory opinion of July 19, 2024, case 186, https://www.icj-cij.org/case/186, addresses the occupied territory and Israel’s continued presence there; its Article 3 finding must not be erased by discussing citizenship within Israel. Assess acts, intent, territorial scope and the applicable legal standard separately.

27. October 7 and competing national claims

UN Special Representative on Sexual Violence in Conflict, mission report of March 4, 2024, summarized at https://press.un.org/en/2024/sc15621.doc.htm, reports reasonable grounds to believe conflict-related sexual violence occurred in several locations on October 7 and clear and convincing information concerning hostages. It distinguishes levels of evidence and reports it could not substantiate. Historical connection, modern nationality and legal entitlement are different propositions. Criticism of Hamas does not establish Palestinian collective guilt; criticism of an Israeli policy does not establish Jewish collective guilt.

28. Institutions, influence and aid

US Department of Justice, Two RT Employees Indicted for Covertly Funding and Directing US Company (September 4, 2024), https://www.jus tice.gov/archives/opa/pr/two-rt-employees-indicted-covertly-funding-and-d irecting-us-company-published-thousands. An indictment states allegations. Obama administration, Fact Sheet: Memorandum of Understanding Reached with Israel (September 14, 2016), https://www.pr esidency.ucsb.edu/documents/fact-sheet-memorandum-understanding-rea ched-with-israel: $38 billion over FY2019–FY2028, subject to congressional appropriations. Human Rights Council session records distinguish its standing agenda item 7 from other UN bodies, resolutions and periods. Counts should state that scope; they do not alone adjudicate the conduct being criticized.

MATHEISM NOTES AND SOURCES

29. Schools, rents and causal comparisons

Hoxby, Murarka and Kang, How New York City’s Charter Schools Affect Achievement (2009), https://users.nber.org/~schools/charterschoolseval/; Dobbie and Fryer, Are High-Quality Schools Enough to Increase Achievement Among the Poor? (2011), https://www.nber.org/papers/w15473. These studies use admission lotteries for specified schools and cohorts. Diamond, McQuade and Qian, The Effects of Rent Control Expansion on Tenants, Landlords, and Inequality (2019), https://www.nber.org/papers/w24181. San Francisco’s expansion protected covered tenants while reducing rental supply; the paper estimates a 15 percent supply reduction among affected properties and a modeled 5.1 percent citywide rent increase. Neither result licenses an identical numerical claim for every school or housing market.

MATHEISM A FEW USEFUL WORDS

## A few useful words

Block universe. Spacetime considered as a complete history. This is distinct from the claim that the laws determine that history uniquely.

Description length. The size of an encoded account in a declared language. A working program need not be the shortest possible program.

Determinism. The specified state and laws fix the evolution. Determinism alone does not select the laws or initial state.

Measure. A rule assigning weights to possibilities. A collection of possible worlds does not choose its own measure.

Mathematical monism. The thesis that reality is entirely mathematical structure, with no additional nonmathematical substance.

One self. The author’s proposal that the bare subject of experience is one across distinct minds; it does not imply shared memories.

Prior and likelihood. A prior weights models before a specified body of evidence. A likelihood says how much probability a model gives that evidence.

Structural exhaustion. The claim that a complete account of constitutive properties and relations leaves no further ingredient to make a thing what it is.

Superdeterminism. Approaches to quantum foundations that allow dependence between measurement settings and the underlying states.

Universal realization. The proposal that every consistent complete mathematical structure exists, with no further stamp of actuality.

Edition record

SO72 T4. 86,646 body words; seven books; 107 chapters. Reordered and revised from the supplied SO70/T510 complete manuscript, with passages from the author’s academic portfolio. The companion assessment records the scope and limits of the review. This is an editorial proposal for the author’s consideration.
